## 2026-07-22 — INCIDENT RESOLVED (D329, ops — no code/grammar change, Forge NOT restarted): 7h producer stall from a D245-class contracts wedge. Operator "Let's restart all the required services"

- **What was wrong:** contracts 1.35.0 (`f5631d7`, Tue 13:56 PDT) added the additive `lot_floor` `SizerSpec.mode` literal. Forge adopted it at the 18:02 v47 restart; the **Crucible fleet had been up since Mon 23:12** holding the pre-1.35.0 model. Inbox ingest is strict `extra="forbid"` → **74 configs rejected** into `inbox/errors/` at 14:25 with `literal_error … input_value='lot_floor'`. Rejected in-flight are in neither `gated_runs` nor `failed_runs` (D245's third category), so batch `1a0f9dc7` sat at 126+74 = a 63% ceiling under the 80% §7.3 bar — the only open batch, so the producer was fully stalled. `crucible-health-check.service` was **failed** since 21:00 naming the cause (`runner_contract_stale`).
- **Fix:** (1) restarted all **10** Crucible long-running units onto 1.35.0 (21:03:53–21:04:08; `db-writer` first — consumers `Requires=` it, the 4 publishers cycled via that dependency); verified `runner_contract_binding contract_version=1.35.0` on both shards. Same-window runner restart also pre-empted the D247 re-read trap. Safe: `validate_schema_version` gates MAJOR only (their pin is still 1.34.0). QuantIQ's 2 Python units were already post-bump + healthy; frontend is Next.js, not a consumer. **Forge itself untouched** (already on 1.35.0; a restart would have cost the in-flight batch for nothing). (2) **REPLAYED** the 74 — `inbox/errors/` preserves the `{config_hash}.json` payloads, all 74 re-validated under 1.35.0, re-submitted via blessed `submit_candidate` (atomic tmp-then-rename), originals kept as backup. **Deviation from D245** (which sentinel-flushed the rows): replay preserves the candidates, mutates no DB row, needs no Forge restart.
- **Verified end-to-end:** inbox 74→0 in ~40s, `errors/` **flat at 3328** (zero re-rejection); `queued` 0→72, `last_decided_age_min` 52→**0.0**, completions all `"source": "forge"`; gated 124→131→143→150→159→**169/200**, bar cleared, feedback consumed (`gated_count=197`), **fresh 200-config batch submitted** ~21:35. Health check re-run **`n_alerts: 0`**. The 3 non-gated remainders are ordinary data-coverage failures (RIVN chain gap) that the D240 flush absorbs.
- **Standing lesson:** a contracts bump must restart the **whole fleet, both directions** — the health monitor's staleness check covers runner shards ONLY, so writer/watchers/publishers/QuantIQ drift is unalerted.
- **RELAYED (07-22):** `PROMPT_CRUCIBLE_CONTRACT_STALENESS_MONITOR_GAP.md` (held, operator carries). Forensics sharpened it: their monitor was **not silent** — `runner_contract_stale` fired every ~15 min for **37 consecutive checks (19:00:45Z → 04:00:02Z, ~9h)**, spanning the whole outage. It was insufficient for two reasons: (1) its own remedy ("restart the shard(s)") would **not** have cleared the wedge, since the culprit was the inbox-watcher — outside the check's 2-of-10 coverage; (2) it fired ~2h **before 1.35.0 was committed**, because the monitor reads `CONTRACT_VERSION` from source (deliberate), so any in-progress `_version.py` edit reports the fleet stale — a routine false positive that trains operators to skip the WARN. Asks: status-write from all 10 consumers (machinery already generalizes — `owner_id` is a free string), CRIT + consequence-naming for the ingest path, optional HEAD-sha to kill the false positive, QuantIQ flagged as a third direction. **Honest symmetry:** Forge's `inbox_rejections` check (D246) catches the consequence, theirs catches the cause — neither was watched.
- **CROSS-REPO (07-22, operator "let's update this"):** bumped Crucible's `CRUCIBLE_EXPECTED_CONTRACT_VERSION` 1.34.0 → **1.35.0** (`5f3e8cc`, one file + version-history comment, pin-only per the 1.34.0 precedent — no `DESIGN.md` row). Their `test_expected_pin_tracks_installed_package` had been RED since the 1.35.0 commit, NO-GOing their `deploy_preflight`; red→green verified their way (`tests/unit` **2737 passed**, ruff + `mypy --strict` clean, all pre-commit gates). **Runtime-inert, no restart** (startup-read constant; MAJOR-only gate; their fleet already on 1.35.0 code since 04:03Z). NOT pushed — their `master` is ahead by 2 and the other commit is their own. Deliberate operator-directed exception to the sibling-agent boundary; their concurrent work (4 modified + 12 untracked) left untouched. Relay's closing section rewritten so it can't be carried stale.
- Also this session: pushed `crucible_contracts` `afbe737..f5631d7` (15 commits, 1.23.0→1.35.0) — Forge pinned 1.35.0 against a version that existed only in the local editable checkout, so the pin is now reproducible from a fresh clone. Forge `main` was already current on origin.

## 2026-07-09 — ranker-eval slowness ROOT-FIXED: vectorized the learned-model trainer with numpy (D260). Verdict IRLS on 250k×106 was ~15 min / 15 G pure-Python single-core; now ~18 s, fp-identical models. [[D260]]

**Operator: "ranker-eval is slow" → profiled it → the cost was the pure-Python fits, not IO/features.**

- **Root cause:** the honest era grew to **N=250,533 rows × 106 features**; the pure-Python Newton-IRLS Hessian is O(N·d²) ≈ 14 B multiply-adds/train. D132 chose pure Python but sized it for a **10k-row window** that `build_dataset` never enforced (25× past premise). `build_dataset` itself is only ~20 s — never the bottleneck.
- **Fix (D260):** numpy-vectorized `_fit_irls` / `_solve_ridge` / `_standardize_design` (linalg only, no RNG — rule-#8-clean). **Scoring untouched → daemon byte-identical.** Same math: old-vs-new on a real 251k snapshot = `max|Δcoef|` 5.6e-13 (verdict) / 9.7e-10 (robustness), metrics identical to 11-13 sig figs.
- **Timing:** full-frame fits **17.6 s (verdict) + 2.6 s (robustness)** vs ~15-18 min pure-Python → the eval's 21-min CPU should drop to ~1 min. Also 15 G → ~0.3 G RAM (ndarray vs list-of-lists).
- **Deploy:** training-only; **no daemon restart** (only the eval trains; daemon scores). numpy added to venv (`pyproject.toml`); committed `a66af5a`. Suite **1866 green** (lone red = concurrent contracts **1.28.0** ivol bump vs 1.27.0 pin — minor, no runtime halt, operator's work — NOT touched).
- **CONFIRMED in production:** manual `forge-ranker-eval` at 23:22Z ran in **1 min 30 s wall** (was ~21 min), CPU 1286 s → 243 s, memory 15.4 G → 11.4 G. Trained+published verdict (auc 0.833) + robustness (r2 0.207) matching validation; `model: fresh 0.0h` clears the D259 CRITICAL. The `wf_p25 drift` WARN persists (latest -0.005) — same known-weak-IC lane, provably NOT from this change (models fp-identical); still a watch item, don't act.

## 2026-07-07 — FLIP DEPLOYED+VERIFIED: exploration holdout MVP (FORGE_EXPLORATION_HOLDOUT_FRAC=0.05, D256, prereg 61837dd2). Last teed-up lever — program COMPLETE. Also fixed 2 pre-existing v24 stale goldens. [[D256]]

**Operator: "keep it at MVP, go ahead." The plain random holdout (not decorrelation-targeted — that would re-introduce bias; targeting decorrelation is an enumeration-floor job).**

- **What:** reserve ~5% of each batch (10/200) as a seeded RANDOM draw from the survivors the ranker didn't pick — REPLACES rank slots (total unchanged), tags them `selection_mode='holdout'`. Composes with gate-tail (gate-tail picks top n−holdout_n, holdout adds the random rest). Cap 0.10; unset=byte-identical. REVERT = drop the line + daemon-reload + restart.
- **Why now:** breaks the censored feedback loop — the F3/wf_p25/estimand models only ever get labels for configs they scored high, so they can't learn where they're wrong. Under gate-tail's hard P-gate that's sharper (ve exclusion could self-reinforce). The holdout keeps unbiased labels (and some ve) flowing regardless of the ranker.
- **Bonus:** stands up a free ranker-vs-random A/B (ranked vs held-out component-rate).
- **Prereg 61837dd2** (cut 22:49Z): ranked comp-rate > held-out comp-rate (ranker beats random) AND overall comp-rate ≥ baseline−0.006. Resolve by selection_mode on the post-flip cohort. Held-out ≥ ranked = ranker red flag.
- **Fraction:** 0.05 (half the cap) — conservative MVP; raise toward 0.10 later if the A/B wants power. Deploy = unit-env ⇒ daemon-reload + restart; verify `exploration_holdout: N of M submitted` in-journal.
- **Levers status:** flip #2 confirmed, flip #1 insufficient/kept, gate-tail live+kept (D255), holdout deploying. **The teed-up lever list is now exhausted.**

## 2026-07-07 — Probe verdict: gate-tail does NOT starve useful ve → KEEP, no carve-out (D255). ve-supply monitor set. gate-tail live on v24; healthcheck OK. [[D255]]

**Investigated the gate-tail ve-share drop (18%→8%) with a probe before deciding — it overturned the revert case.**

- **The correction:** I'd read ve's *realized* component-rate (0.003) as its *predicted* P. Wrong. Probe on `shadow_scores.model_score` (logged per-config P): ve's predicted P has real spread (median 0.024, max 0.32), **54.7% clears the 0.02 gate** — gate-tail selects *within* ve, doesn't exclude the family.
- **Decisive probe:** of **485 realized ve COMPONENTS, 90.3% clear the 0.02 gate** (median P 0.139); ve REJECTS have P median 0.0099 (39% clear). So P(component) predicts ve clearance — gate-tail keeps ~90% of the ve that matter and cuts the reject-bound ve. **Carve-out NOT necessary; no revert.**
- **Decision: KEEP gate-tail** (running on v24, `GATE-TAIL ACTIVE` p_floor=0.02 verified in-journal, coexists with the operator's v24 grammar — orthogonal ranking vs enumeration).

## 2026-07-02 — ve `|move|` null (e1a43ba8) DROPPED: Crucible quality read finds NO support + INVERTS the thesis (more-magnitude ve = MORE correlated). Prereg → refuted. Book-PBO baseline corrected 0.107 → 0.178. [[D235]]

**Operator forwarded `FORGE_ve_move_null_selection_quality_RESULT_2026-07-02.md` (Crucible's answer to my quality prompt).** The only thing keeping `e1a43ba8` shelved-not-dropped was: is the |move| cut a quality tightening or a supply throttle?
- **Crucible's read (622 honest single-name ve comps, all classifiable):** (1) the cheap realized-P&L proxy CAN'T isolate the signal-level |move| partition — 100% realize as directional (0/622 reach magnitude 0.5) because a |move|-signal through a directional OTM structure → delta-$ P&L (structure confound; **blocker is proxy dispersion, NOT sample size**). (2) the weak within-band gradient **INVERTS the thesis**: more-magnitude ve (Q5) is MORE correlated (|corr| 0.070→0.118) + loads HIGHER on PC1 (0.066→0.104) = LESS decorrelated; marginal_sharpe only mildly favors Q5. → **no support for "magnitude-selected ve is higher-quality."**
- **DECISION: DROP e1a43ba8.** Refuted on survival (D226 −62%) + quality-unsupported/thesis-inverted (this read) + redundant (flip-1 `848a1f67` +2.2× and the D216 floor already supply ve). Prereg `e1a43ba8ee14` resolved **refuted**. The airtight route (build the exact `config_hash → ve_null_profile` tag for a Crucible re-score) stays available on request but is low-EV. No code change (flag was already OFF/byte-identical).
- **BASELINE CORRECTION (propagate):** the promotable book's CSCV PBO is **0.178** (10-group/45-path confirm), NOT the 0.107 I'd been citing (8-group/28-path est). **Still clears 0.40 / below the 0.50 floor — still promotable.** Corrected in memory.
- **POSTURE: unchanged; daemon untouched.** Teed-up 07-04 levers: `848a1f67` (flip), `5082d332` (flip), `9063b405` (flip, SPRT-gated), `FORGE_EXPLORATION_HOLDOUT_FRAC` (activate). `e1a43ba8` retired.

## 2026-07-02 — Strategy-audit P1-2b SHIPPED flag-OFF: signal_correlation excludes the regime_filter GATE — un-throttles event families. Measured: 94% of ve kills are the gate co-firing structurally, not redundancy. Byte-identical; flip pre-registered. [[D227]]

**Operator chose "un-throttle event families." P1-2(b): signal_correlation kills 20% of multi-signal ve configs (vs 2-6% elsewhere). Measured the mechanism on live data FIRST (PRE-H3's "measure before changing"), and it's a category error, not a threshold miss.**
- **Measurement (live, no code):** 293k live signal_correlation rejects → 55% Jaccard ≥0.95, **88% involve `sig_regime`**. Live-cache verify (N=1500, `scripts/signal_correlation_regime_pair_audit.py`): ve **65/320 rejected (20%), 61 (94%) are regime-pairs** (median Jaccard 0.949) on **event-calendar gates** (days_to_nfp/cpi/fomc/opex). Genuine content-pair redundancy is rare (9) + marginal (0.869).
- **Root cause:** `sig_regime` has role `regime_filter` — a CONTEXT GATE, not an alpha signal. The gate co-firing with the signals it gates is STRUCTURAL (a non-binding gate), not the "two edges = one" redundancy the filter exists to catch. Penalizing it is a category error.
- **P1-2b (`902e04a`, 4 tests):** new flag `signal_correlation.exclude_regime_filter` (default **False** → byte-identical). When True, only alpha-bearing signals (non-regime_filter) are compared → gate↔content co-firing no longer rejects, content↔content still does. **Role-aware, NOT family-aware** — the mechanism spans all families (relval 100%, trend 100%, mr 72% regime-pair), so excluding the gate is narrower + more principled than a ve threshold (refines the workplan's (b), logged as D227). Invariants + 396 tests green; daemon untouched.
- **Prereg `5082d332b26e`:** post-flip ve signal_correlation reject fraction falls ~0.20 → content-only ~0.01-0.02, ve survivors rise, no book-PBO harm; keep if confirmed else revert. **Flip = feedback-change, operator-gated, after D220.** Independent of the D224/D225 permutation flips (different filter).
- **POSTURE: flag-OFF, NOT flipped; daemon untouched.** **Completes strategy-audit P1-2:** (a)=D225 ve |move| [REFUTED by the D226 shadow-count], (b)=this [VALIDATED by measurement, ready to flip], (c)=predicted_activations [control, no change]. **RESUME:** P1-3/P1-4 are grammar bumps (operator-gated) — or the learned-systems tier (principled P blend). Still hold live-stream changes until D220 `b7ecc2d2` resolves (≥07-04).

(2) `evaluate_shadow` (`cc844c2`) emits `model_ece` (overall — toothless here, 0.018), **`model_max_ce`** (the floor-relevant max gap over populated bins — live **0.356 → FAIL**), `model_ece_platt` (held-out Platt = the reachable floor, **0.006** → miscalibration is RECOVERABLE); centralized `shadow_auc_verdict` + new `shadow_calibration_verdict`. (3) `RewireEvaluation.eligible_fraction` (`d255e4e`) — the gate-tail floor KEEP-RATE (live **0.9869** at floor 0.02). (4) `forge status` (`2ec6d51`) — a `P calibration/floor` drift line. `eval` + `eval-rewire` + the daily timer print + JSONL-track all of it.
- **Why max_ce not overall ECE:** the P mass sits in well-calibrated low bins (median ~0.0004) so overall ECE is ~0 even while the high-P sliver the floor SELECTS is 3-5× off; max_ce over ≥20-row bins captures exactly the miscalibration that bites. **Why two verdicts:** AUC blesses RANKING (blend); calibration blesses the ABSOLUTE floor read — kept separate so a well-ranking-but-miscalibrated model isn't wrongly failed. **No live behavior change** (telemetry; daemon untouched; base fit byte-identical, hard rule #6).
- **Deferred to P1.1 (operator-gated at flip):** persist the `(a,b)` in the artifact + re-derive the `FORGE_REWIRE_P_FLOOR` on the calibrated scale + wire calibrated-P into the gate-tail lane. The recalibrator's application is inseparable from the floor re-derivation, so it lands there.
- **RESUME PLAN:** **P1.1** (Task #14) — close the gate-then-tail shadow-vs-production fidelity gap (make production hard-gate below-floor before the composite under gate-tail mode; flag-OFF byte-identical; parity test) AND fold in the P1.3 recalibrator (persist `(a,b)` + re-derive the floor). Ungated to build; still don't deploy a ranking change that moves the submitted component-rate until the D220 prereg resolves (≥2026-07-04T08Z). Then P1.2/P2 per the audit.

## 2026-07-02 — Prereg `b7ecc2d2e96f` EARLY READ (D220 prior-weight raise): +8.5h post-cut, realized component-rate **0.117 vs ~0.078 recent baseline** — prediction (≥0.06) holds decisively. Kitagawa split: **72% of the lift is within-family ranker skill** (the intended P effect, robust to the trend↔mr oscillation); vol_event floor ruled out as confounder. **Keep 0.50 live; hold prereg open for ≥48h formal resolution.** Next: P1.3 (calibrate P(component)).

**Prereg check (fresh `/tmp` snapshot, cut 08:02:09Z, now 16:38Z ≈ 8.5h post-cut; 7,314 of 7,400 post-cut submissions decided — near-complete, not decision-lag-censored).** Component-rate (`decision IN component,promote`) by `submitted_at` (attributes to the live ranker weight): **post-cut 0.1174 vs pre-cut(all) 0.0777**; recent trailing windows −24h..cut **0.078**, −48..−24h 0.056, −72..−48h 0.064, −7d..−72h 0.090 → the recent baseline is ~0.06–0.09 and post-cut clears every one.
- **Confounder controls (the decisive part):** (1) **vol_event floor ruled OUT** — its component-rate is ~zero (0.0046→0.0019) and share barely moved (0.061→0.072), so D216 is NOT driving the lift. (2) **Kitagawa decomposition of the +0.0391 aggregate lift: rate effect (within-family ranker skill) +0.0283 (72%), mix effect (composition/oscillation) +0.0109 (28%).** Within-family rates rose in BOTH cores (trend 0.121→0.164, mr 0.045→0.060) — not explainable by the trend↔mr mix oscillation → clean evidence the higher F3-prior weight is selecting better configs. **Even discounting the entire 28% mix effect as the oscillation confounder, the residual rate lift alone → ~0.106, still » the ≥0.06 prediction.**
- **VERDICT: strong early confirmation; prereg HELD OPEN (not resolved) for ≥48h** to confirm across a full trend↔mr cycle before formal resolution + the keep-vs-revert call. 0.50 stays live (current state, evidence supports it; revert only triggers on failure). Re-run this decomposition on a fresh snapshot ≥2026-07-04T08Z. Probe: `scratchpad/` (gitignored; method recorded here).
